NOTIFICATION 

Caloutta , the 6th March 1954 
In pursuance of Rule 18 of the Rules made by the Government of India under section 28 of the Public 
Debt Act, 1944 and published in the Gazette of India of the 20th April 1946 the following list is hereby advertised of securities 
lost eto, in respect of which prima facie grounds exist for believing that the socuritios have boen lost and that the claim of the 
applicants is just. All porsons other than the rospective claimants nained below who have any olaim , upou those securities , 
should communicate immediately with the Manager, Reserve Bank of India , Calcutta . 

The list is divided into two parts : — Part A being the list of the so curities now advertised for the first time, and Part 
B the list of securities previously advertised .
